Also out of scope for every Goal unless explicitly named here:
- bypassing Source Gate for real data;
- creating microservices/distributed infrastructure merely for future scale;
- replacing the existing event/commit/replay authority with a new subsystem;
- weakening earlier tests to make the new feature pass.

## 6. Architecture Constraints

1. `Canonical World State` remains authoritative; this Goal may submit commands/observations/proposed deltas but may not create a second mutation path.
2. All new domain semantics live behind cohesive modules and stable ports. External libraries belong in adapters.
3. `domain` remains independent of FastAPI, SQLAlchemy/Alembic, frontend frameworks and model-provider SDKs.
4. Persisted/public types are versioned; migrations and compatibility behavior are explicit.
5. Deterministic core tests do not require network, credentials or an LLM.
6. Projection/session/adapter state never becomes world truth merely because it exists in a client or external system.
7. Rights, provenance, knowledge isolation and audit metadata are enforced at the earliest relevant boundary.
8. Default production source-file target remains approximately <=300 lines. Split by responsibility rather than hiding code in generic `manager.py`, `service.py`, `utils.py` or `helpers.py`.
9. No global mutable singleton, hidden constructor I/O, swallowed exceptions or ORM session leakage.
10. New behavior must be observable with structured identifiers and reproducible from documented fixtures.
11. If a new plugin/extension can execute code, apply the trusted/untrusted package policy; default to no arbitrary executable code from untrusted packages.
12. A new adapter may fail or be unavailable without corrupting or partially mutating canonical state.

## 7. Deliverables

- Production implementation for Completion Ledger & Review Workflow under existing modular-monolith boundaries.
- Typed public/domain/application contracts with explicit version metadata where persisted or externally consumed.
- Deterministic synthetic fixtures and fakes for every external boundary introduced by this Goal.
- Goal-specific automated tests plus regression coverage for all earlier milestone invariants touched by the change.
- `reports/g04d_report.md` with commands, results, acceptance evidence, limitations and changed files.
- Ledger/ADR updates for non-trivial architecture or compatibility decisions.

## 8. Implementation Tasks

Execute all tasks; split implementation internally into cohesive work packages rather than one large file:

1. Define content truth/status taxonomy consistent with master spec.
2. Implement review use-cases and immutable decision history.
3. Implement canon lock and override/branch rules.
4. Require completion rationale/provenance and reviewer identity where applicable.
5. Expose diff between candidate/reviewed package versions.
6. Add conflicts and revised-decision fixtures.
7. Integrate with compiler/package registry.

Additional mandatory work:
- inspect existing abstractions before adding new ones; reuse only if semantics match;
- add explicit errors for invalid/unsupported/conflict states;
- add schema/version metadata at first persistence/public exposure;
- add audit/trace data for important decisions and mutations;
- update generated API/SDK schemas when relevant;
- run a changed-files cohesion review before declaring PASS.

## 9. Tests

Goal-specific required tests:

- unit: status transitions and invalid promotions
- negative: model inference cannot become canon without required review/evidence
- history: reversal preserves old decision
- rights: review cannot approve disallowed redistribution
- audit: every compiled package item exposes truth label

Global regression tests required when applicable:
- M1 Commit/Event/Replay/Branch/Idempotency/StaleRevision suite;
- architecture dependency guards;
- migration compatibility;
- rights/projection leakage;
- deterministic replay and semantic hash;
- persistence restart;
- negative/error-path assertions.

Tests must prove behavior, not file existence. Mocks may isolate external systems, but acceptance must exercise real internal code paths.

## 10. Acceptance Criteria

- All implementation tasks in G04D are complete; no acceptance-critical TODO/FIXME/placeholder/NotImplemented/mock-only production path remains.
- All Goal-specific tests PASS and all previously passing milestone gates remain green.
- Ruff/lint, type checking, architecture conformance and applicable frontend/build checks PASS.
- Any persisted/public schema change has a migration/compatibility test and does not silently break old replay/package data.
- Any canonical mutation introduced by this Goal still flows through Validate/Resolve/Commit and produces auditable events.
- No new direct dependency from domain/core to FastAPI, SQLAlchemy, UI, model SDK or concrete external adapter is introduced.
- Goal evidence report exists and the final local Git checkpoint is created only after PASS.

Acceptance status for each criterion must be one of `PASS`, `FAIL`, `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED`, `NOT_APPLICABLE`, with evidence.

## 11. Failure / Blocker Handling

- Treat implementation defects, test failures, type errors, design inconsistencies that can be resolved from the master specification, missing internal migrations and architecture violations as **internal work**. Fix them.
- Use `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` only for genuinely unavailable external data, authorization, credential, service, hardware or engine binary that the Goal explicitly depends on.
- When an external item is blocked, still complete the interface, fake/fixture, validation, error handling, documentation and all internal acceptance possible.
- A real-data reference pack being `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` must not block a generic platform milestone if the milestone gate explicitly permits that path.
- Never fabricate real historical/literary/family/museum facts to avoid a blocker.
- If a blocker reveals a contradiction in the master spec that changes product semantics, document it in `BLOCKERS.md` and stop only the affected branch of work; continue independent Goals if safe.

## 12. Documentation Updates

Before PASS:
- update `STATUS.md` and `PLAN.md`;
- append important compatibility/architecture choices to `DECISIONS.md` or ADRs;
- update `BLOCKERS.md` and `KNOWN_FAILURES.md`;
- add a concise `CHANGELOG.md` entry;
- update `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md`;
- update API/package/interop/runbook docs touched by this Goal;
- write `reports/g04d_report.md` with reproducible commands and evidence.

## 13. Git / Checkpoint Requirements

After all acceptance criteria PASS or permitted external-only items are explicitly documented:
1. ensure the working tree contains only intended changes;
2. run the full Goal gate one final time;
3. create a local commit:
   `goal g04d: completion ledger & review workflow`
4. record the commit hash in the Goal report and `STATUS.md`;
5. do not push/deploy unless the user separately requests it;
6. continue automatically to the next Goal.

If the Goal is the last Goal before a milestone, do not mark the milestone PASS yet. Run the milestone qualification document first.

## 1. Objective

Implement safe control handoff so human takeover pauses primary AI policy, optional ShadowPolicy assists without competing, and AI resumes with all consequences.

## 2. Dependencies / Entry Conditions

This Goal may start only when the following are true:

- G05B

Do not infer missing requirements from model memory or old chats.

## 4. Scope

- HumanPolicy
- ShadowPolicy
- handoff state
- resume context
- micro-expression/advice-only scopes
- policy ownership audit

## 5. Non-goals

- Do not let ShadowPolicy choose canonical actions while human owns body.
- Do not reset actor memory/state on return.
- Do not require an LLM.

Also out of scope for every Goal unless explicitly named here:
- bypassing Source Gate for real data;
- creating microservices/distributed infrastructure merely for future scale;
- replacing the existing event/commit/replay authority with a new subsystem;
- weakening earlier tests to make the new feature pass.

## 6. Architecture Constraints

1. `Canonical World State` remains authoritative; this Goal may submit commands/observations/proposed deltas but may not create a second mutation path.
2. All new domain semantics live behind cohesive modules and stable ports. External libraries belong in adapters.

7. Rights, provenance, knowledge isolation and audit metadata are enforced at the earliest relevant boundary.
8. Default production source-file target remains approximately <=300 lines. Split by responsibility rather than hiding code in generic `manager.py`, `service.py`, `utils.py` or `helpers.py`.
9. No global mutable singleton, hidden constructor I/O, swallowed exceptions or ORM session leakage.

## 8. Implementation Tasks

Execute all tasks; split implementation internally into cohesive work packages rather than one large file:

1. Define control handoff state machine.
2. Pause autonomous actor controller on lease acquisition.
3. Allow shadow outputs only in explicitly non-authoritative assistance channels.
4. Record committed human actions as normal world history.
5. On release, restore AI/deterministic controller using current world+memory state.
6. Test interrupted skill/schedule behavior during takeover.
7. Add synthetic takeover/resume scenario.

- relevant existing source, tests, migrations, ADRs and the previous Goal report

Do not infer missing requirements from model memory or old chats.

## 4. Scope

- map projection
- actor/object tokens
- movement/interaction command submission
- projection refresh/stream
- basic camera/focus
- semantic location mapping

## 5. Non-goals

- Do not implement gameplay rules in Phaser.
- Do not use rendered coordinates as canonical topology.
- Do not create a separate save format.

Also out of scope for every Goal unless explicitly named here:
- bypassing Source Gate for real data;
- creating microservices/distributed infrastructure merely for future scale;
- replacing the existing event/commit/replay authority with a new subsystem;
- weakening earlier tests to make the new feature pass.

## 6. Architecture Constraints

1. `Canonical World State` remains authoritative; this Goal may submit commands/observations/proposed deltas but may not create a second mutation path.
2. All new domain semantics live behind cohesive modules and stable ports. External libraries belong in adapters.
3. `domain` remains independent of FastAPI, SQLAlchemy/Alembic, frontend frameworks and model-provider SDKs.
4. Persisted/public types are versioned; migrations and compatibility behavior are explicit.
5. Deterministic core tests do not require network, credentials or an LLM.
6. Projection/session/adapter state never becomes world truth merely because it exists in a client or external system.
7. Rights, provenance, knowledge isolation and audit metadata are enforced at the earliest relevant boundary.
8. Default production source-file target remains approximately <=300 lines. Split by responsibility rather than hiding code in generic `manager.py`, `service.py`, `utils.py` or `helpers.py`.
9. No global mutable singleton, hidden constructor I/O, swallowed exceptions or ORM session leakage.
10. New behavior must be observable with structured identifiers and reproducible from documented fixtures.
11. If a new plugin/extension can execute code, apply the trusted/untrusted package policy; default to no arbitrary executable code from untrusted packages.
12. A new adapter may fail or be unavailable without corrupting or partially mutating canonical state.

## 7. Deliverables

- Production implementation for Phaser 2D Player Vertical Slice under existing modular-monolith boundaries.
- Typed public/domain/application contracts with explicit version metadata where persisted or externally consumed.
- Deterministic synthetic fixtures and fakes for every external boundary introduced by this Goal.
- Goal-specific automated tests plus regression coverage for all earlier milestone invariants touched by the change.
- `reports/g05f_report.md` with commands, results, acceptance evidence, limitations and changed files.
- Ledger/ADR updates for non-trivial architecture or compatibility decisions.

## 8. Implementation Tasks

Execute all tasks; split implementation internally into cohesive work packages rather than one large file:

1. Create Phaser scene fed by projection API.
2. Map semantic places/portals to display coordinates via projection metadata.
3. Submit movement/interaction commands through generated client.
4. Render rejected actions and branch/session context.
5. Ensure reconnect reconstructs view from server projection.
6. Add deterministic e2e test with synthetic map.
7. Document renderer independence.

Additional mandatory work:
- inspect existing abstractions before adding new ones; reuse only if semantics match;
- add explicit errors for invalid/unsupported/conflict states;
- add schema/version metadata at first persistence/public exposure;
- add audit/trace data for important decisions and mutations;
- update generated API/SDK schemas when relevant;
- run a changed-files cohesion review before declaring PASS.

## 9. Tests

Goal-specific required tests:

- build/typecheck
- e2e: move via valid portal and observe authoritative result
- negative: locked portal remains rejected despite client attempt
- reconnect: client state restored from projection
- cross-view: Studio and Phaser observe same committed world

Global regression tests required when applicable:
- M1 Commit/Event/Replay/Branch/Idempotency/StaleRevision suite;
- architecture dependency guards;
- migration compatibility;
- rights/projection leakage;
- deterministic replay and semantic hash;
- persistence restart;
- negative/error-path assertions.

Tests must prove behavior, not file existence. Mocks may isolate external systems, but acceptance must exercise real internal code paths.

## 10. Acceptance Criteria

- All implementation tasks in G05F are complete; no acceptance-critical TODO/FIXME/placeholder/NotImplemented/mock-only production path remains.
- All Goal-specific tests PASS and all previously passing milestone gates remain green.
- Ruff/lint, type checking, architecture conformance and applicable frontend/build checks PASS.
- Any persisted/public schema change has a migration/compatibility test and does not silently break old replay/package data.
- Any canonical mutation introduced by this Goal still flows through Validate/Resolve/Commit and produces auditable events.
- No new direct dependency from domain/core to FastAPI, SQLAlchemy, UI, model SDK or concrete external adapter is introduced.
- Goal evidence report exists and the final local Git checkpoint is created only after PASS.

Acceptance status for each criterion must be one of `PASS`, `FAIL`, `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED`, `NOT_APPLICABLE`, with evidence.

## 11. Failure / Blocker Handling

- Treat implementation defects, test failures, type errors, design inconsistencies that can be resolved from the master specification, missing internal migrations and architecture violations as **internal work**. Fix them.
- Use `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` only for genuinely unavailable external data, authorization, credential, service, hardware or engine binary that the Goal explicitly depends on.
- When an external item is blocked, still complete the interface, fake/fixture, validation, error handling, documentation and all internal acceptance possible.
- A real-data reference pack being `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` must not block a generic platform milestone if the milestone gate explicitly permits that path.
- Never fabricate real historical/literary/family/museum facts to avoid a blocker.
- If a blocker reveals a contradiction in the master spec that changes product semantics, document it in `BLOCKERS.md` and stop only the affected branch of work; continue independent Goals if safe.

## 12. Documentation Updates

Before PASS:
- update `STATUS.md` and `PLAN.md`;
- append important compatibility/architecture choices to `DECISIONS.md` or ADRs;
- update `BLOCKERS.md` and `KNOWN_FAILURES.md`;
- add a concise `CHANGELOG.md` entry;
- update `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md`;
- update API/package/interop/runbook docs touched by this Goal;
- write `reports/g05f_report.md` with reproducible commands and evidence.

## 13. Git / Checkpoint Requirements

After all acceptance criteria PASS or permitted external-only items are explicitly documented:
1. ensure the working tree contains only intended changes;
2. run the full Goal gate one final time;
3. create a local commit:

4. record the commit hash in the Goal report and `STATUS.md`;
5. do not push/deploy unless the user separately requests it;
6. continue automatically to the next Goal.

- `05_CODEX_REMAINING_PROGRAM_MASTER_PROMPT.md`
- `06_REMAINING_GOALS_INDEX.md`
- `07_MILESTONE_GATES_M2_M9.md`
- current `PLAN.md`, `STATUS.md`, `DECISIONS.md`, `BLOCKERS.md`, `KNOWN_FAILURES.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`
- relevant existing source, tests, migrations, ADRs and the previous Goal report

Do not infer missing requirements from model memory or old chats.

## 4. Scope

- WorldDirector
- NarrativeDirector
- PerformanceDirector
- Hard Constraints
- Narrative Goals
- Attractors
- Free Space
- scene/focus/external event proposal
- character-delta candidate review

## 5. Non-goals

- Do not force love/hate/belief/personality changes.
- Do not allow director direct commit.
- Do not make cinematics modify world facts.

Also out of scope for every Goal unless explicitly named here:
- bypassing Source Gate for real data;
- creating microservices/distributed infrastructure merely for future scale;
- replacing the existing event/commit/replay authority with a new subsystem;
- weakening earlier tests to make the new feature pass.

## 6. Architecture Constraints

1. `Canonical World State` remains authoritative; this Goal may submit commands/observations/proposed deltas but may not create a second mutation path.
2. All new domain semantics live behind cohesive modules and stable ports. External libraries belong in adapters.
3. `domain` remains independent of FastAPI, SQLAlchemy/Alembic, frontend frameworks and model-provider SDKs.
4. Persisted/public types are versioned; migrations and compatibility behavior are explicit.
5. Deterministic core tests do not require network, credentials or an LLM.
6. Projection/session/adapter state never becomes world truth merely because it exists in a client or external system.
7. Rights, provenance, knowledge isolation and audit metadata are enforced at the earliest relevant boundary.
8. Default production source-file target remains approximately <=300 lines. Split by responsibility rather than hiding code in generic `manager.py`, `service.py`, `utils.py` or `helpers.py`.
9. No global mutable singleton, hidden constructor I/O, swallowed exceptions or ORM session leakage.
10. New behavior must be observable with structured identifiers and reproducible from documented fixtures.
11. If a new plugin/extension can execute code, apply the trusted/untrusted package policy; default to no arbitrary executable code from untrusted packages.
12. A new adapter may fail or be unavailable without corrupting or partially mutating canonical state.

## 7. Deliverables

- Production implementation for Director Runtime under existing modular-monolith boundaries.
- Typed public/domain/application contracts with explicit version metadata where persisted or externally consumed.
- Deterministic synthetic fixtures and fakes for every external boundary introduced by this Goal.
- Goal-specific automated tests plus regression coverage for all earlier milestone invariants touched by the change.
- `reports/g07d_report.md` with commands, results, acceptance evidence, limitations and changed files.
- Ledger/ADR updates for non-trivial architecture or compatibility decisions.

## 8. Implementation Tasks

Execute all tasks; split implementation internally into cohesive work packages rather than one large file:

1. Define director contracts and ownership boundaries.
2. Implement deterministic reference world director for focus/time/event scheduling.
3. Implement narrative goals/attractors as scoring/proposal signals.
4. Implement performance directives as projection-only metadata.
5. Validate every world-changing director proposal through normal action/event path.
6. Require actor-logic review for character/persona delta candidates.
7. Trace director version, inputs and influence.

Additional mandatory work:
- inspect existing abstractions before adding new ones; reuse only if semantics match;
- add explicit errors for invalid/unsupported/conflict states;
- add schema/version metadata at first persistence/public exposure;
- add audit/trace data for important decisions and mutations;
- update generated API/SDK schemas when relevant;
- run a changed-files cohesion review before declaring PASS.

## 9. Tests

Goal-specific required tests:

- unit: constraint precedence
- negative: hard constraint violation rejected
- authority: director lacks commit port
- projection: performance directive changes rendering metadata only
- character: proposed mental change cannot bypass cognition/persona policy

Global regression tests required when applicable:
- M1 Commit/Event/Replay/Branch/Idempotency/StaleRevision suite;
- architecture dependency guards;
- migration compatibility;
- rights/projection leakage;
- deterministic replay and semantic hash;
- persistence restart;
- negative/error-path assertions.

Tests must prove behavior, not file existence. Mocks may isolate external systems, but acceptance must exercise real internal code paths.

## 10. Acceptance Criteria

- All implementation tasks in G07D are complete; no acceptance-critical TODO/FIXME/placeholder/NotImplemented/mock-only production path remains.
- All Goal-specific tests PASS and all previously passing milestone gates remain green.
- Ruff/lint, type checking, architecture conformance and applicable frontend/build checks PASS.
- Any persisted/public schema change has a migration/compatibility test and does not silently break old replay/package data.
- Any canonical mutation introduced by this Goal still flows through Validate/Resolve/Commit and produces auditable events.
- No new direct dependency from domain/core to FastAPI, SQLAlchemy, UI, model SDK or concrete external adapter is introduced.
- Goal evidence report exists and the final local Git checkpoint is created only after PASS.

Acceptance status for each criterion must be one of `PASS`, `FAIL`, `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED`, `NOT_APPLICABLE`, with evidence.

## 11. Failure / Blocker Handling

- Treat implementation defects, test failures, type errors, design inconsistencies that can be resolved from the master specification, missing internal migrations and architecture violations as **internal work**. Fix them.
- Use `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` only for genuinely unavailable external data, authorization, credential, service, hardware or engine binary that the Goal explicitly depends on.
- When an external item is blocked, still complete the interface, fake/fixture, validation, error handling, documentation and all internal acceptance possible.
- A real-data reference pack being `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` must not block a generic platform milestone if the milestone gate explicitly permits that path.
- Never fabricate real historical/literary/family/museum facts to avoid a blocker.
- If a blocker reveals a contradiction in the master spec that changes product semantics, document it in `BLOCKERS.md` and stop only the affected branch of work; continue independent Goals if safe.

## 12. Documentation Updates

Before PASS:
- update `STATUS.md` and `PLAN.md`;
- append important compatibility/architecture choices to `DECISIONS.md` or ADRs;
- update `BLOCKERS.md` and `KNOWN_FAILURES.md`;
- add a concise `CHANGELOG.md` entry;
- update `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md`;
- update API/package/interop/runbook docs touched by this Goal;
- write `reports/g07d_report.md` with reproducible commands and evidence.

## 13. Git / Checkpoint Requirements

After all acceptance criteria PASS or permitted external-only items are explicitly documented:
1. ensure the working tree contains only intended changes;
2. run the full Goal gate one final time;
3. create a local commit:
   `goal g07d: director runtime`
4. record the commit hash in the Goal report and `STATUS.md`;
5. do not push/deploy unless the user separately requests it;
6. continue automatically to the next Goal.

If the Goal is the last Goal before a milestone, do not mark the milestone PASS yet. Run the milestone qualification document first.

- migration compatibility;
- rights/projection leakage;
- deterministic replay and semantic hash;

Tests must prove behavior, not file existence. Mocks may isolate external systems, but acceptance must exercise real internal code paths.

- If a blocker reveals a contradiction in the master spec that changes product semantics, document it in `BLOCKERS.md` and stop only the affected branch of work; continue independent Goals if safe.

## 12. Documentation Updates

Before editing production code, rerun the narrow regression suite for the dependency boundary. If a prior milestone regression is discovered, repair the regression first and record it; do not build new capability on a broken base.

## 3. Required Reading

Read before implementation:

- `docs/spec/WANXIANG_v5_MASTER_SPEC.md`, especially:
  - §14–15 literature/history reference instances
  - §16–25 Family World
  - §26–37 Heritage/Museum
  - §49–52 domain evaluation
- `00_WANXIANG_ENGINEERING_PROGRAM_ARCHITECTURE.md`
- `02_ENGINEERING_STANDARDS.md`
- `03_ACCEPTANCE_TESTING_AND_EVIDENCE.md`
- `05_CODEX_REMAINING_PROGRAM_MASTER_PROMPT.md`
- `06_REMAINING_GOALS_INDEX.md`
- `07_MILESTONE_GATES_M2_M9.md`
- current `PLAN.md`, `STATUS.md`, `DECISIONS.md`, `BLOCKERS.md`, `KNOWN_FAILURES.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`
- relevant existing source, tests, migrations, ADRs and the previous Goal report

Do not infer missing requirements from model memory or old chats.

## 4. Scope

- IIIF Manifest
- Canvas/AnnotationPage/Annotation bodies as needed
- external IDs
- image/audio/video service refs
- rights/attribution
- local metadata cache/provenance

## 5. Non-goals

- Do not scrape or redistribute media beyond rights.
- Do not make IIIF object itself the semantic heritage twin.
- Do not require downloading large binaries for core tests.

Also out of scope for every Goal unless explicitly named here:
- bypassing Source Gate for real data;
- creating microservices/distributed infrastructure merely for future scale;
- replacing the existing event/commit/replay authority with a new subsystem;
- weakening earlier tests to make the new feature pass.

## 6. Architecture Constraints

1. `Canonical World State` remains authoritative; this Goal may submit commands/observations/proposed deltas but may not create a second mutation path.
2. All new domain semantics live behind cohesive modules and stable ports. External libraries belong in adapters.
3. `domain` remains independent of FastAPI, SQLAlchemy/Alembic, frontend frameworks and model-provider SDKs.
4. Persisted/public types are versioned; migrations and compatibility behavior are explicit.
5. Deterministic core tests do not require network, credentials or an LLM.
6. Projection/session/adapter state never becomes world truth merely because it exists in a client or external system.
7. Rights, provenance, knowledge isolation and audit metadata are enforced at the earliest relevant boundary.
8. Default production source-file target remains approximately <=300 lines. Split by responsibility rather than hiding code in generic `manager.py`, `service.py`, `utils.py` or `helpers.py`.
9. No global mutable singleton, hidden constructor I/O, swallowed exceptions or ORM session leakage.
10. New behavior must be observable with structured identifiers and reproducible from documented fixtures.
11. If a new plugin/extension can execute code, apply the trusted/untrusted package policy; default to no arbitrary executable code from untrusted packages.
12. A new adapter may fail or be unavailable without corrupting or partially mutating canonical state.

## 7. Deliverables

- Production implementation for IIIF Ingest under existing modular-monolith boundaries.
- Typed public/domain/application contracts with explicit version metadata where persisted or externally consumed.
- Deterministic synthetic fixtures and fakes for every external boundary introduced by this Goal.
- Goal-specific automated tests plus regression coverage for all earlier milestone invariants touched by the change.
- `reports/g10a_report.md` with commands, results, acceptance evidence, limitations and changed files.
- Ledger/ADR updates for non-trivial architecture or compatibility decisions.

## 8. Implementation Tasks

Execute all tasks; split implementation internally into cohesive work packages rather than one large file:

1. Define IIIF adapter and normalized media reference model.
2. Parse supported manifest profile with explicit compatibility.
3. Preserve canonical external IDs, labels, rights/attribution and source URL metadata.
4. Map annotations/media to heritage asset candidates/evidence references.
5. Implement export/reference round-trip where supported.
6. Use local fixture manifests for deterministic tests.
7. Add malformed/rights-denied fixture.

Additional mandatory work:
- inspect existing abstractions before adding new ones; reuse only if semantics match;
- add explicit errors for invalid/unsupported/conflict states;
- add schema/version metadata at first persistence/public exposure;
- add audit/trace data for important decisions and mutations;
- update generated API/SDK schemas when relevant;
- run a changed-files cohesion review before declaring PASS.

## 9. Tests

Goal-specific required tests:

- fixture parse/round-trip
- rights metadata preserved
- no network required in core tests
- unsupported IIIF feature diagnosed explicitly
- source provenance retained

Global regression tests required when applicable:
- M1 Commit/Event/Replay/Branch/Idempotency/StaleRevision suite;
- architecture dependency guards;
- migration compatibility;
- rights/projection leakage;
- deterministic replay and semantic hash;
- persistence restart;
- negative/error-path assertions.

Tests must prove behavior, not file existence. Mocks may isolate external systems, but acceptance must exercise real internal code paths.

## 10. Acceptance Criteria

- All implementation tasks in G10A are complete; no acceptance-critical TODO/FIXME/placeholder/NotImplemented/mock-only production path remains.
- All Goal-specific tests PASS and all previously passing milestone gates remain green.
- Ruff/lint, type checking, architecture conformance and applicable frontend/build checks PASS.
- Any persisted/public schema change has a migration/compatibility test and does not silently break old replay/package data.
- Any canonical mutation introduced by this Goal still flows through Validate/Resolve/Commit and produces auditable events.
- No new direct dependency from domain/core to FastAPI, SQLAlchemy, UI, model SDK or concrete external adapter is introduced.
- Goal evidence report exists and the final local Git checkpoint is created only after PASS.

Acceptance status for each criterion must be one of `PASS`, `FAIL`, `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED`, `NOT_APPLICABLE`, with evidence.

## 11. Failure / Blocker Handling

- Treat implementation defects, test failures, type errors, design inconsistencies that can be resolved from the master specification, missing internal migrations and architecture violations as **internal work**. Fix them.
- Use `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` only for genuinely unavailable external data, authorization, credential, service, hardware or engine binary that the Goal explicitly depends on.
- When an external item is blocked, still complete the interface, fake/fixture, validation, error handling, documentation and all internal acceptance possible.
- A real-data reference pack being `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` must not block a generic platform milestone if the milestone gate explicitly permits that path.
- Never fabricate real historical/literary/family/museum facts to avoid a blocker.
- If a blocker reveals a contradiction in the master spec that changes product semantics, document it in `BLOCKERS.md` and stop only the affected branch of work; continue independent Goals if safe.

## 12. Documentation Updates

Before PASS:
- update `STATUS.md` and `PLAN.md`;
- append important compatibility/architecture choices to `DECISIONS.md` or ADRs;
- update `BLOCKERS.md` and `KNOWN_FAILURES.md`;
- add a concise `CHANGELOG.md` entry;
- update `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md`;
- update API/package/interop/runbook docs touched by this Goal;
- write `reports/g10a_report.md` with reproducible commands and evidence.

## 13. Git / Checkpoint Requirements

After all acceptance criteria PASS or permitted external-only items are explicitly documented:
1. ensure the working tree contains only intended changes;
2. run the full Goal gate one final time;
3. create a local commit:
   `goal g10a: iiif ingest`
4. record the commit hash in the Goal report and `STATUS.md`;
5. do not push/deploy unless the user separately requests it;
6. continue automatically to the next Goal.

If the Goal is the last Goal before a milestone, do not mark the milestone PASS yet. Run the milestone qualification document first.

Also out of scope for every Goal unless explicitly named here:

## 6. Architecture Constraints

8. Default production source-file target remains approximately <=300 lines. Split by responsibility rather than hiding code in generic `manager.py`, `service.py`, `utils.py` or `helpers.py`.
9. No global mutable singleton, hidden constructor I/O, swallowed exceptions or ORM session leakage.
10. New behavior must be observable with structured identifiers and reproducible from documented fixtures.
11. If a new plugin/extension can execute code, apply the trusted/untrusted package policy; default to no arbitrary executable code from untrusted packages.
12. A new adapter may fail or be unavailable without corrupting or partially mutating canonical state.

> Execution mode: continuous; on PASS create checkpoint and continue automatically.  
> Product source of truth: `docs/spec/WANXIANG_v5_MASTER_SPEC.md`

## 1. Objective

Implement campaign mechanisms that stress organization orders, logistics, movement constraints and epistemic isolation.

## 2. Dependencies / Entry Conditions

This Goal may start only when the following are true:

- G11C

Before editing production code, rerun the narrow regression suite for the dependency boundary. If a prior milestone regression is discovered, repair the regression first and record it; do not build new capability on a broken base.

## 3. Required Reading

Read before implementation:

- `docs/spec/WANXIANG_v5_MASTER_SPEC.md`, especially:
  - §5.4 Co-Simulation Fabric
  - §15 Liaoshen reference instance
  - Appendix D §33 Experiment Runtime
  - Appendix E §51 simulation verification/validation
- `00_WANXIANG_ENGINEERING_PROGRAM_ARCHITECTURE.md`
- `02_ENGINEERING_STANDARDS.md`
- `03_ACCEPTANCE_TESTING_AND_EVIDENCE.md`
- `05_CODEX_REMAINING_PROGRAM_MASTER_PROMPT.md`
- `06_REMAINING_GOALS_INDEX.md`
- `07_MILESTONE_GATES_M2_M9.md`
- current `PLAN.md`, `STATUS.md`, `DECISIONS.md`, `BLOCKERS.md`, `KNOWN_FAILURES.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`
- relevant existing source, tests, migrations, ADRs and the previous Goal report

Do not infer missing requirements from model memory or old chats.

## 4. Scope

- order delay/lifecycle
- supply/resource flow
- route capacity
- movement time
- observation/intelligence
- fog-of-war/beliefs
- communication failure/deviation

## 5. Non-goals

- Do not create unrestricted real-world tactical optimization advice.
- Do not give actors omniscient canonical state.
- Do not bypass common material/spatial systems.

Also out of scope for every Goal unless explicitly named here:
- bypassing Source Gate for real data;
- creating microservices/distributed infrastructure merely for future scale;
- replacing the existing event/commit/replay authority with a new subsystem;
- weakening earlier tests to make the new feature pass.

## 6. Architecture Constraints

1. `Canonical World State` remains authoritative; this Goal may submit commands/observations/proposed deltas but may not create a second mutation path.
2. All new domain semantics live behind cohesive modules and stable ports. External libraries belong in adapters.
3. `domain` remains independent of FastAPI, SQLAlchemy/Alembic, frontend frameworks and model-provider SDKs.
4. Persisted/public types are versioned; migrations and compatibility behavior are explicit.
5. Deterministic core tests do not require network, credentials or an LLM.
6. Projection/session/adapter state never becomes world truth merely because it exists in a client or external system.
7. Rights, provenance, knowledge isolation and audit metadata are enforced at the earliest relevant boundary.
8. Default production source-file target remains approximately <=300 lines. Split by responsibility rather than hiding code in generic `manager.py`, `service.py`, `utils.py` or `helpers.py`.
9. No global mutable singleton, hidden constructor I/O, swallowed exceptions or ORM session leakage.
10. New behavior must be observable with structured identifiers and reproducible from documented fixtures.
11. If a new plugin/extension can execute code, apply the trusted/untrusted package policy; default to no arbitrary executable code from untrusted packages.
12. A new adapter may fail or be unavailable without corrupting or partially mutating canonical state.

## 7. Deliverables

- Production implementation for Command, Logistics, Movement & Fog-of-war under existing modular-monolith boundaries.
- Typed public/domain/application contracts with explicit version metadata where persisted or externally consumed.
- Deterministic synthetic fixtures and fakes for every external boundary introduced by this Goal.
- Goal-specific automated tests plus regression coverage for all earlier milestone invariants touched by the change.
- `reports/g11d_report.md` with commands, results, acceptance evidence, limitations and changed files.
- Ledger/ADR updates for non-trivial architecture or compatibility decisions.

## 8. Implementation Tasks

Execute all tasks; split implementation internally into cohesive work packages rather than one large file:

1. Implement campaign order execution through organization runtime.
2. Implement synthetic logistics transfers/capacity/consumption.
3. Implement movement using spatial/path/time constraints.
4. Generate observations/intelligence with uncertainty and perspective.
5. Maintain faction-specific beliefs/fog-of-war.
6. Integrate co-sim proposals where applicable.
7. Record all assumptions/rule versions.

Additional mandatory work:
- inspect existing abstractions before adding new ones; reuse only if semantics match;
- add explicit errors for invalid/unsupported/conflict states;
- add schema/version metadata at first persistence/public exposure;
- add audit/trace data for important decisions and mutations;
- update generated API/SDK schemas when relevant;
- run a changed-files cohesion review before declaring PASS.

## 9. Tests

Goal-specific required tests:

- logistics conservation
- orders delayed/rejected as rules dictate
- fog-of-war prevents future/hidden knowledge leakage
- replay same seed produces same synthetic outcome
- negative: impossible movement/supply transfer rejected

Global regression tests required when applicable:
- M1 Commit/Event/Replay/Branch/Idempotency/StaleRevision suite;
- architecture dependency guards;
- migration compatibility;
- rights/projection leakage;
- deterministic replay and semantic hash;
- persistence restart;
- negative/error-path assertions.

Tests must prove behavior, not file existence. Mocks may isolate external systems, but acceptance must exercise real internal code paths.

## 10. Acceptance Criteria

- All implementation tasks in G11D are complete; no acceptance-critical TODO/FIXME/placeholder/NotImplemented/mock-only production path remains.
- All Goal-specific tests PASS and all previously passing milestone gates remain green.
- Ruff/lint, type checking, architecture conformance and applicable frontend/build checks PASS.
- Any persisted/public schema change has a migration/compatibility test and does not silently break old replay/package data.
- Any canonical mutation introduced by this Goal still flows through Validate/Resolve/Commit and produces auditable events.
- No new direct dependency from domain/core to FastAPI, SQLAlchemy, UI, model SDK or concrete external adapter is introduced.
- Goal evidence report exists and the final local Git checkpoint is created only after PASS.

Acceptance status for each criterion must be one of `PASS`, `FAIL`, `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED`, `NOT_APPLICABLE`, with evidence.

## 11. Failure / Blocker Handling

- Treat implementation defects, test failures, type errors, design inconsistencies that can be resolved from the master specification, missing internal migrations and architecture violations as **internal work**. Fix them.
- Use `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` only for genuinely unavailable external data, authorization, credential, service, hardware or engine binary that the Goal explicitly depends on.
- When an external item is blocked, still complete the interface, fake/fixture, validation, error handling, documentation and all internal acceptance possible.
- A real-data reference pack being `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` must not block a generic platform milestone if the milestone gate explicitly permits that path.
- Never fabricate real historical/literary/family/museum facts to avoid a blocker.
- If a blocker reveals a contradiction in the master spec that changes product semantics, document it in `BLOCKERS.md` and stop only the affected branch of work; continue independent Goals if safe.

## 12. Documentation Updates

Before PASS:
- update `STATUS.md` and `PLAN.md`;
- append important compatibility/architecture choices to `DECISIONS.md` or ADRs;
- update `BLOCKERS.md` and `KNOWN_FAILURES.md`;
- add a concise `CHANGELOG.md` entry;
- update `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md`;
- update API/package/interop/runbook docs touched by this Goal;
- write `reports/g11d_report.md` with reproducible commands and evidence.

## 13. Git / Checkpoint Requirements

After all acceptance criteria PASS or permitted external-only items are explicitly documented:
1. ensure the working tree contains only intended changes;
2. run the full Goal gate one final time;
3. create a local commit:
   `goal g11d: command, logistics, movement & fog-of-war`
4. record the commit hash in the Goal report and `STATUS.md`;
5. do not push/deploy unless the user separately requests it;
6. continue automatically to the next Goal.

If the Goal is the last Goal before a milestone, do not mark the milestone PASS yet. Run the milestone qualification document first.

1. Define reproducible long-run benchmark fixture.
2. Run at least 30 in-world days and 1000+ scheduler/commit cycles.
3. Measure latency distribution, event growth, snapshot size, queue depth and memory.
4. Verify no invariant/rights/knowledge/branch violations.
5. Replay from multiple checkpoints to final semantic hash.
6. Profile hotspots and fix pathological growth while preserving architecture.
7. Record baseline thresholds and regression budget.

Additional mandatory work:
- inspect existing abstractions before adding new ones; reuse only if semantics match;
- add explicit errors for invalid/unsupported/conflict states;
- add schema/version metadata at first persistence/public exposure;
- add audit/trace data for important decisions and mutations;
- update generated API/SDK schemas when relevant;
- run a changed-files cohesion review before declaring PASS.

## 9. Tests

Goal-specific required tests:

- full long-run deterministic PASS
- same seed final semantic hash stable
- no unbounded scheduler/memory leak beyond documented policy
- replay from checkpoints matches
- performance report with machine/environment context

Global regression tests required when applicable:
- M1 Commit/Event/Replay/Branch/Idempotency/StaleRevision suite;
- architecture dependency guards;
- migration compatibility;
- rights/projection leakage;
- deterministic replay and semantic hash;
- persistence restart;
- negative/error-path assertions.

Tests must prove behavior, not file existence. Mocks may isolate external systems, but acceptance must exercise real internal code paths.

## 10. Acceptance Criteria

- All implementation tasks in G12A are complete; no acceptance-critical TODO/FIXME/placeholder/NotImplemented/mock-only production path remains.
- All Goal-specific tests PASS and all previously passing milestone gates remain green.
- Ruff/lint, type checking, architecture conformance and applicable frontend/build checks PASS.
- Any persisted/public schema change has a migration/compatibility test and does not silently break old replay/package data.
- Any canonical mutation introduced by this Goal still flows through Validate/Resolve/Commit and produces auditable events.
- No new direct dependency from domain/core to FastAPI, SQLAlchemy, UI, model SDK or concrete external adapter is introduced.
- Goal evidence report exists and the final local Git checkpoint is created only after PASS.

Acceptance status for each criterion must be one of `PASS`, `FAIL`, `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED`, `NOT_APPLICABLE`, with evidence.

## 11. Failure / Blocker Handling

- Treat implementation defects, test failures, type errors, design inconsistencies that can be resolved from the master specification, missing internal migrations and architecture violations as **internal work**. Fix them.
- Use `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` only for genuinely unavailable external data, authorization, credential, service, hardware or engine binary that the Goal explicitly depends on.
- When an external item is blocked, still complete the interface, fake/fixture, validation, error handling, documentation and all internal acceptance possible.
- A real-data reference pack being `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` must not block a generic platform milestone if the milestone gate explicitly permits that path.
- Never fabricate real historical/literary/family/museum facts to avoid a blocker.
- If a blocker reveals a contradiction in the master spec that changes product semantics, document it in `BLOCKERS.md` and stop only the affected branch of work; continue independent Goals if safe.

## 12. Documentation Updates

Before PASS:
- update `STATUS.md` and `PLAN.md`;
- append important compatibility/architecture choices to `DECISIONS.md` or ADRs;
- update `BLOCKERS.md` and `KNOWN_FAILURES.md`;
- add a concise `CHANGELOG.md` entry;
- update `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md`;
- update API/package/interop/runbook docs touched by this Goal;
- write `reports/g12a_report.md` with reproducible commands and evidence.

## 13. Git / Checkpoint Requirements

After all acceptance criteria PASS or permitted external-only items are explicitly documented:
1. ensure the working tree contains only intended changes;
2. run the full Goal gate one final time;
3. create a local commit:
   `goal g12a: 30-day / 1000+ tick stability qualification`
4. record the commit hash in the Goal report and `STATUS.md`;
5. do not push/deploy unless the user separately requests it;
6. continue automatically to the next Goal.

If the Goal is the last Goal before a milestone, do not mark the milestone PASS yet. Run the milestone qualification document first.

> Phase: P9 — Release Qualification and Advanced Adapters  
> Target milestone: M9  
> Execution mode: continuous; on PASS create checkpoint and continue automatically.  
> Product source of truth: `docs/spec/WANXIANG_v5_MASTER_SPEC.md`

## 1. Objective

Qualify operational durability across backups, restores, schema migrations and version upgrades.

## 2. Dependencies / Entry Conditions

This Goal may start only when the following are true:

- G12A

Before editing production code, rerun the narrow regression suite for the dependency boundary. If a prior milestone regression is discovered, repair the regression first and record it; do not build new capability on a broken base.

## 3. Required Reading

Read before implementation:

- `docs/spec/WANXIANG_v5_MASTER_SPEC.md`, especially:
  - §8.2 Projection Gateway
  - §9.4 Asset Foundry
  - Appendix E §§43–59 interoperability/security/engineering
  - G12 roadmap and evaluation sections
- `00_WANXIANG_ENGINEERING_PROGRAM_ARCHITECTURE.md`
- `02_ENGINEERING_STANDARDS.md`
- `03_ACCEPTANCE_TESTING_AND_EVIDENCE.md`
- `05_CODEX_REMAINING_PROGRAM_MASTER_PROMPT.md`
- `06_REMAINING_GOALS_INDEX.md`
- `07_MILESTONE_GATES_M2_M9.md`
- current `PLAN.md`, `STATUS.md`, `DECISIONS.md`, `BLOCKERS.md`, `KNOWN_FAILURES.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`
- relevant existing source, tests, migrations, ADRs and the previous Goal report

Do not infer missing requirements from model memory or old chats.

## 4. Scope

- SQLite backup/restore
- PostgreSQL-compatible migration path
- object/package assets references
- event/snapshot integrity
- migration rollback/forward policy
- disaster recovery runbook

## 5. Non-goals

- Do not claim PostgreSQL runtime verification if environment cannot run it.
- Do not make backup a raw copy without integrity checks.
- Do not silently drop old events.

Also out of scope for every Goal unless explicitly named here:
- bypassing Source Gate for real data;
- creating microservices/distributed infrastructure merely for future scale;
- replacing the existing event/commit/replay authority with a new subsystem;
- weakening earlier tests to make the new feature pass.

## 6. Architecture Constraints

1. `Canonical World State` remains authoritative; this Goal may submit commands/observations/proposed deltas but may not create a second mutation path.
2. All new domain semantics live behind cohesive modules and stable ports. External libraries belong in adapters.
3. `domain` remains independent of FastAPI, SQLAlchemy/Alembic, frontend frameworks and model-provider SDKs.
4. Persisted/public types are versioned; migrations and compatibility behavior are explicit.
5. Deterministic core tests do not require network, credentials or an LLM.
6. Projection/session/adapter state never becomes world truth merely because it exists in a client or external system.
7. Rights, provenance, knowledge isolation and audit metadata are enforced at the earliest relevant boundary.
8. Default production source-file target remains approximately <=300 lines. Split by responsibility rather than hiding code in generic `manager.py`, `service.py`, `utils.py` or `helpers.py`.
9. No global mutable singleton, hidden constructor I/O, swallowed exceptions or ORM session leakage.
10. New behavior must be observable with structured identifiers and reproducible from documented fixtures.
11. If a new plugin/extension can execute code, apply the trusted/untrusted package policy; default to no arbitrary executable code from untrusted packages.
12. A new adapter may fail or be unavailable without corrupting or partially mutating canonical state.

## 7. Deliverables

- Production implementation for Backup, Restore & Migration Qualification under existing modular-monolith boundaries.
- Typed public/domain/application contracts with explicit version metadata where persisted or externally consumed.
- Deterministic synthetic fixtures and fakes for every external boundary introduced by this Goal.
- Goal-specific automated tests plus regression coverage for all earlier milestone invariants touched by the change.
- `reports/g12b_report.md` with commands, results, acceptance evidence, limitations and changed files.
- Ledger/ADR updates for non-trivial architecture or compatibility decisions.

## 8. Implementation Tasks

Execute all tasks; split implementation internally into cohesive work packages rather than one large file:

1. Implement consistent backup command/process for local deployment.
2. Validate backup manifest/content hashes and package references.
3. Restore into clean environment and rerun semantic/replay checks.
4. Run migration chain from oldest retained fixture DB to current.
5. Test old event/snapshot/package compatibility.
6. Add optional PostgreSQL integration path when Docker/service available; otherwise mark only that environment check EXTERNAL_BLOCKED.
7. Document RPO/RTO expectations for supported deployment modes.

Additional mandatory work:
- inspect existing abstractions before adding new ones; reuse only if semantics match;
- add explicit errors for invalid/unsupported/conflict states;

## 9. Tests

# G12E — Godot / Babylon Projection Contracts

> Phase: P9 — Release Qualification and Advanced Adapters  
> Target milestone: M9  
> Execution mode: continuous; on PASS create checkpoint and continue automatically.  
> Product source of truth: `docs/spec/WANXIANG_v5_MASTER_SPEC.md`

## 1. Objective

Provide engine-neutral 2D/3D projection/gateway contracts and minimal examples for Godot and Babylon without creating alternate world authorities.

## 2. Dependencies / Entry Conditions

This Goal may start only when the following are true:

- G12D

Before editing production code, rerun the narrow regression suite for the dependency boundary. If a prior milestone regression is discovered, repair the regression first and record it; do not build new capability on a broken base.

## 3. Required Reading

Read before implementation:

- `docs/spec/WANXIANG_v5_MASTER_SPEC.md`, especially:
  - §8.2 Projection Gateway
  - §9.4 Asset Foundry
  - Appendix E §§43–59 interoperability/security/engineering
  - G12 roadmap and evaluation sections
- `00_WANXIANG_ENGINEERING_PROGRAM_ARCHITECTURE.md`
- `02_ENGINEERING_STANDARDS.md`
- `03_ACCEPTANCE_TESTING_AND_EVIDENCE.md`
- `05_CODEX_REMAINING_PROGRAM_MASTER_PROMPT.md`
- `06_REMAINING_GOALS_INDEX.md`
- `07_MILESTONE_GATES_M2_M9.md`
- current `PLAN.md`, `STATUS.md`, `DECISIONS.md`, `BLOCKERS.md`, `KNOWN_FAILURES.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`
- relevant existing source, tests, migrations, ADRs and the previous Goal report

Do not infer missing requirements from model memory or old chats.

## 4. Scope

- scene/entity transform projection
- asset refs
- animation/state cues
- command input
- network/update protocol
- glTF/OpenUSD/3D Tiles refs as metadata

## 5. Non-goals

- Do not implement game rules in engines.
- Do not require full native engine CI if unavailable.
- Do not turn transforms into canonical topology by default.

Also out of scope for every Goal unless explicitly named here:
- bypassing Source Gate for real data;
- creating microservices/distributed infrastructure merely for future scale;
- replacing the existing event/commit/replay authority with a new subsystem;
- weakening earlier tests to make the new feature pass.

## 6. Architecture Constraints

1. `Canonical World State` remains authoritative; this Goal may submit commands/observations/proposed deltas but may not create a second mutation path.
2. All new domain semantics live behind cohesive modules and stable ports. External libraries belong in adapters.
3. `domain` remains independent of FastAPI, SQLAlchemy/Alembic, frontend frameworks and model-provider SDKs.
4. Persisted/public types are versioned; migrations and compatibility behavior are explicit.
5. Deterministic core tests do not require network, credentials or an LLM.
6. Projection/session/adapter state never becomes world truth merely because it exists in a client or external system.
7. Rights, provenance, knowledge isolation and audit metadata are enforced at the earliest relevant boundary.
8. Default production source-file target remains approximately <=300 lines. Split by responsibility rather than hiding code in generic `manager.py`, `service.py`, `utils.py` or `helpers.py`.
9. No global mutable singleton, hidden constructor I/O, swallowed exceptions or ORM session leakage.
10. New behavior must be observable with structured identifiers and reproducible from documented fixtures.
11. If a new plugin/extension can execute code, apply the trusted/untrusted package policy; default to no arbitrary executable code from untrusted packages.
12. A new adapter may fail or be unavailable without corrupting or partially mutating canonical state.

## 7. Deliverables

- Production implementation for Godot / Babylon Projection Contracts under existing modular-monolith boundaries.
- Typed public/domain/application contracts with explicit version metadata where persisted or externally consumed.
- Deterministic synthetic fixtures and fakes for every external boundary introduced by this Goal.
- Goal-specific automated tests plus regression coverage for all earlier milestone invariants touched by the change.
- `reports/g12e_report.md` with commands, results, acceptance evidence, limitations and changed files.
- Ledger/ADR updates for non-trivial architecture or compatibility decisions.

## 8. Implementation Tasks

Execute all tasks; split implementation internally into cohesive work packages rather than one large file:

1. Define versioned 3D projection schema.
2. Map semantic world entities/places/assets to render descriptors.
3. Provide Babylon web example or schema consumer where feasible.
4. Provide Godot protocol/example files without requiring engine binary for core tests.
5. Ensure commands return through API/host.
6. Add contract/golden tests for projection payload.
7. Document renderer-specific non-authoritative state.

Additional mandatory work:
- inspect existing abstractions before adding new ones; reuse only if semantics match;
- add explicit errors for invalid/unsupported/conflict states;
- add schema/version metadata at first persistence/public exposure;
- add audit/trace data for important decisions and mutations;
- update generated API/SDK schemas when relevant;
- run a changed-files cohesion review before declaring PASS.

## 9. Tests

Goal-specific required tests:

- projection schema tests
- rights-filtered asset references
- command roundtrip via host in available example
- core CI independent of Godot binary
- no renderer state needed for replay

Global regression tests required when applicable:
- M1 Commit/Event/Replay/Branch/Idempotency/StaleRevision suite;
- architecture dependency guards;
- migration compatibility;
- rights/projection leakage;
- deterministic replay and semantic hash;
- persistence restart;
- negative/error-path assertions.

Tests must prove behavior, not file existence. Mocks may isolate external systems, but acceptance must exercise real internal code paths.

## 10. Acceptance Criteria

- All implementation tasks in G12E are complete; no acceptance-critical TODO/FIXME/placeholder/NotImplemented/mock-only production path remains.
- All Goal-specific tests PASS and all previously passing milestone gates remain green.
- Ruff/lint, type checking, architecture conformance and applicable frontend/build checks PASS.
- Any persisted/public schema change has a migration/compatibility test and does not silently break old replay/package data.
- Any canonical mutation introduced by this Goal still flows through Validate/Resolve/Commit and produces auditable events.
- No new direct dependency from domain/core to FastAPI, SQLAlchemy, UI, model SDK or concrete external adapter is introduced.
- Goal evidence report exists and the final local Git checkpoint is created only after PASS.

Acceptance status for each criterion must be one of `PASS`, `FAIL`, `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED`, `NOT_APPLICABLE`, with evidence.

## 11. Failure / Blocker Handling

- Treat implementation defects, test failures, type errors, design inconsistencies that can be resolved from the master specification, missing internal migrations and architecture violations as **internal work**. Fix them.
- Use `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` only for genuinely unavailable external data, authorization, credential, service, hardware or engine binary that the Goal explicitly depends on.
- When an external item is blocked, still complete the interface, fake/fixture, validation, error handling, documentation and all internal acceptance possible.
- A real-data reference pack being `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` must not block a generic platform milestone if the milestone gate explicitly permits that path.
- Never fabricate real historical/literary/family/museum facts to avoid a blocker.
- If a blocker reveals a contradiction in the master spec that changes product semantics, document it in `BLOCKERS.md` and stop only the affected branch of work; continue independent Goals if safe.

## 12. Documentation Updates

Before PASS:
- update `STATUS.md` and `PLAN.md`;
- append important compatibility/architecture choices to `DECISIONS.md` or ADRs;
- update `BLOCKERS.md` and `KNOWN_FAILURES.md`;
- add a concise `CHANGELOG.md` entry;
- update `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md`;
- update API/package/interop/runbook docs touched by this Goal;
- write `reports/g12e_report.md` with reproducible commands and evidence.

## 13. Git / Checkpoint Requirements

After all acceptance criteria PASS or permitted external-only items are explicitly documented:
1. ensure the working tree contains only intended changes;
2. run the full Goal gate one final time;
3. create a local commit:
   `goal g12e: godot / babylon projection contracts`
4. record the commit hash in the Goal report and `STATUS.md`;
5. do not push/deploy unless the user separately requests it;
6. continue automatically to the next Goal.

If the Goal is the last Goal before a milestone, do not mark the milestone PASS yet. Run the milestone qualification document first.

- Deterministic synthetic fixtures and fakes for every external boundary introduced by this Goal.
- Goal-specific automated tests plus regression coverage for all earlier milestone invariants touched by the change.
- `reports/g12h_report.md` with commands, results, acceptance evidence, limitations and changed files.
- Ledger/ADR updates for non-trivial architecture or compatibility decisions.

## 8. Implementation Tasks

Execute all tasks; split implementation internally into cohesive work packages rather than one large file:

1. Harden configuration and secret loading.
2. Add upload/file validation and source prompt-injection isolation checks.
3. Add least-privilege authorization around admin/debug/private memory/export operations.
4. Protect audit history from ordinary deletion paths.
5. Provide Docker Compose and no-Docker local run instructions.
6. Add health/readiness and structured observability surfaces.
7. Run dependency/license/secret/security checks available in toolchain.
8. Produce threat model, private-deployment runbook and M9 release checklist.

Additional mandatory work:
- inspect existing abstractions before adding new ones; reuse only if semantics match;
- add explicit errors for invalid/unsupported/conflict states;
- add schema/version metadata at first persistence/public exposure;
- add audit/trace data for important decisions and mutations;
- update generated API/SDK schemas when relevant;
- run a changed-files cohesion review before declaring PASS.

## 9. Tests

Goal-specific required tests:

- fresh clone/install/start smoke test
- security negative tests for unauthorized admin/private/export operations
- secret scan clean
- backup/restore/migration suite green
- complete regression across M1-M8 acceptance matrices
- M9 release report enumerates external-blocked adapters/data without calling them complete

Global regression tests required when applicable:
- M1 Commit/Event/Replay/Branch/Idempotency/StaleRevision suite;
- architecture dependency guards;
- migration compatibility;
- rights/projection leakage;
- deterministic replay and semantic hash;
- persistence restart;
- negative/error-path assertions.

Tests must prove behavior, not file existence. Mocks may isolate external systems, but acceptance must exercise real internal code paths.

## 10. Acceptance Criteria

- All implementation tasks in G12H are complete; no acceptance-critical TODO/FIXME/placeholder/NotImplemented/mock-only production path remains.
- All Goal-specific tests PASS and all previously passing milestone gates remain green.
- Ruff/lint, type checking, architecture conformance and applicable frontend/build checks PASS.
- Any persisted/public schema change has a migration/compatibility test and does not silently break old replay/package data.
- Any canonical mutation introduced by this Goal still flows through Validate/Resolve/Commit and produces auditable events.
- No new direct dependency from domain/core to FastAPI, SQLAlchemy, UI, model SDK or concrete external adapter is introduced.
- Goal evidence report exists and the final local Git checkpoint is created only after PASS.

Acceptance status for each criterion must be one of `PASS`, `FAIL`, `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED`, `NOT_APPLICABLE`, with evidence.

## 11. Failure / Blocker Handling

- Treat implementation defects, test failures, type errors, design inconsistencies that can be resolved from the master specification, missing internal migrations and architecture violations as **internal work**. Fix them.
- Use `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` only for genuinely unavailable external data, authorization, credential, service, hardware or engine binary that the Goal explicitly depends on.
- When an external item is blocked, still complete the interface, fake/fixture, validation, error handling, documentation and all internal acceptance possible.
- A real-data reference pack being `EXTERNAL_BLOCKED` must not block a generic platform milestone if the milestone gate explicitly permits that path.
- Never fabricate real historical/literary/family/museum facts to avoid a blocker.
- If a blocker reveals a contradiction in the master spec that changes product semantics, document it in `BLOCKERS.md` and stop only the affected branch of work; continue independent Goals if safe.

## 12. Documentation Updates

Before PASS:
- update `STATUS.md` and `PLAN.md`;
- append important compatibility/architecture choices to `DECISIONS.md` or ADRs;
- update `BLOCKERS.md` and `KNOWN_FAILURES.md`;
- add a concise `CHANGELOG.md` entry;
- update `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md`;
- update API/package/interop/runbook docs touched by this Goal;
- write `reports/g12h_report.md` with reproducible commands and evidence.

## 13. Git / Checkpoint Requirements

After all acceptance criteria PASS or permitted external-only items are explicitly documented:
1. ensure the working tree contains only intended changes;
2. run the full Goal gate one final time;
3. create a local commit:
   `goal g12h: deployment, security & private installation qualification`
4. record the commit hash in the Goal report and `STATUS.md`;
5. do not push/deploy unless the user separately requests it;
6. continue automatically to the next Goal.

If the Goal is the last Goal before a milestone, do not mark the milestone PASS yet. Run the milestone qualification document first.
